**2026-07-21 — TODAY ORB — MOBILE SAFARI PARITY (MEASUREMENT ONLY, no code change) — CLASS A (product boundary) → HOLD.** Finding: installed app Today shows the Orb; Safari Today does not. **ROOT CAUSE (measured, not guessed):** the visible Orb is `OrbLiving`, rendered in EXACTLY ONE production surface — `/start/page.client.tsx:128` (the native launch "Threshold Door"). It is NOT in BtyDailyAppShell's Today tab (that tab renders aurora/seed-bloom + the small `WeeklyOrb`, a different component). `/start` routes native→`/{locale}/app`, web→`/{locale}/today` (page.client.tsx:62). Post-3E.3 Safari enters via `/`→middleware→`/{locale}/app`, **bypassing `/start` entirely** → the launch Orb never plays. **Not an isNative/platform gate on the Orb visual** (OrbLiving has NO native render gate — only `triggerHaptic` is native-conditional, correct), **not asset/CSP** (canvas-2D, self-contained, no external asset; renders on the public web `/{locale}/dev/orb` route), **not perf** (canvas-2D + `prefers-reduced-motion` path + CSS-gradient fallback on `getContext` failure → never blank). **Classification: A (intentional product boundary by ENTRY PATH)** — the Orb is the native app-open ritual; Safari enters the shell directly by the design 3E.3 just finalized. Browser parity was never part of the Orb spec, though the component is web-capable. **Recommendation: HOLD** (no defect to fix). If product WANTS Safari Orb parity, the narrowest additive path (separate future slice, not this task) = render a one-time `OrbLiving` threshold beat on the web shell's first Today open (component already web-safe; haptics already native-conditional) — without redesigning the Orb or touching routing/auth/Supabase/native. Files that WOULD be affected by such a slice: `src/components/app-shell/BtyDailyAppShell.tsx` (Today first-open beat) reusing `src/components/orb/OrbLiving.tsx` as-is. No code changed in this measurement task.

**2026-07-17 — Foundry ADAPTIVE CLARIFICATION (Slice 2.4C) — deterministic sufficiency gate between direction-selection (2.4A) and the Module Draft Copilot (2.4B); asks the SMALLEST clarification only when a named dimension is materially under-specified, one question at a time, zero for well-described directions; NO migration; 2.4B CLOSED (PASS); Create Arena practice remains a handoff; DEPLOYED to bty-arena-staging; COMMANDER IPHONE PRODUCT GATE PENDING.**
[Adaptive Clarification 2.4C] PRODUCT: after the Host applies a 2.4A direction and the Draft Copilot's canonical minimum context is complete (problem+audience+behavior+evidence), tapping "Draft the rest of this training" first consults a DETERMINISTIC sufficiency verdict. Sufficient (the common case) → straight into the EXISTING 2.4B generate→review→apply flow, ZERO questions. Under-specified → the smallest clarification, ONE question at a time, reassessed after each answer, stopping the instant it becomes sufficient; hard ceiling 3 (a safety cap, not a target); Host may "Draft with what I have" at any point (authorship preserved). STEP-0 MEASURED (no STOP): insertion point = the front door of `ModuleDraftCopilot` idle→generate (NOT a new wizard, NOT a replacement of the 2.4B review UI); persistence owner UNCHANGED = the `foundry_module_drafts.answers` jsonb (single source of truth, shallow-merged by `updateDraftStep`, restored on mount) → clarification state rides a NAMESPACED `answers.clarification` key, excluded from `SNAPSHOT_ANSWER_KEYS` (never publishes) and never overwriting a canonical Builder field → **NO migration, no second source of truth, resume-safe**. ARCHITECTURE: sufficiency/deficiency is a PURE domain contract (sole progression authority); AI never decides progression — it only enriches the downstream draft. Implementation (inner-main, mirrors 2.4A/2.4B layering): (1) domain `clarification.ts` — `ClarificationDimension` (7 modeled, extensible) + `assessClarification(ctx,state)→{sufficient,missingDimensions,nextQuestion,askedCount}`; conservative deterministic detectors, priority-ordered by DOWNSTREAM IMPACT (v1 activates the two highest-impact material ones: `observable_behavior` reusing `observableBehaviorWarning` [vague phrase / <4 words], `success_evidence` [<4 words]); answered dimensions excluded from re-asking regardless of unchanged canonical text; ceiling forces sufficient at 3; `readClarificationState`/`withClarificationAnswer`/`clarificationsForContext` (resume-safe sanitize, last-valid-per-dimension); NO display strings (UI copy localizes by dimension + choice key). (2) `module-builder.ts` — narrow additive `clarification?` on `BuilderAnswers` + bounded structural validation in `validateDraftPatch` (`clarification_invalid`; ≤6 answers, ≤300-char text). (3) service `moduleDraftCopilotService.generateModuleDraft(ctx,locale,clarifications=[])` — Host clarifications appended to the user prompt as authoritative extra context (never in the stale fingerprint; the model enriches, never gates). (4) route `POST /modules/[id]/module-draft` — reads clarifications from the SERVER draft via `clarificationsForContext` and passes them through; zero mutation, unchanged canonical fingerprint/stale-guard. (5) UI `ModuleDraftCopilot.tsx` — new `clarifying` phase: trigger→(sufficient? generate : ask); one localized question at a time (2–4 suggested chips where genuinely helpful [evidence uses seen/heard/recorded/confirmed], custom always permitted); answer persists via canonical `patchAnswers({clarification},true)`; verdict recomputed each render from ctx + persisted state; flips-to-sufficient auto-advances to generate; internal confidence never exposed. Shell wires `assessClarification(moduleCtx, readClarificationState(answers))` + `answerClarification`. NOT Dr. Chi voice. SCOPE BOUNDARIES HONORED: no Arena scenario/Practice Pack generation, no publish-behavior change, no canonical-schema change beyond the narrow namespaced key, existing 2.4A + 2.4B behavior unchanged, "Create Arena practice" remains a recommendation/handoff only. Verification: `tsc` clean; **+21 tests** (domain clarification 11: zero-question sufficient, one-question named-dimension, priority order, reassess-after-answer, answered-never-reasked, ceiling, resume-safe sanitize; persistence 3; component 6: sufficient-skips, insufficient-asks-first, choice-captured-no-canonical-write, custom-trimmed, draft-anyway, flip-to-sufficient-auto-generates; route 1: clarifications passed to generation); Foundry+Arena suite **892** green; full suite only the 16 documented pre-existing failures (4932 pass) — ZERO new regressions; ZERO new terminology violations (git A/B: 28=28); build OK. **2026-07-17 — Arena PRACTICE CHOICE GHOST-CAPSULE FIX (Slice 2.4A.4) — the first DECIDE choice's unexplained right-side pill (leaked isActionCommitment) removed; label-only cards, uniform structure; DEPLOYED to bty-arena-staging; COMMANDER ARENA CHOICE VISUAL GATE PENDING.**
[Arena Choice Ghost 2.4A.4] Commander real device (after full Foundry→publish→Arena play): before any selection, the FIRST choice on the three-choice DECIDE screen showed a small right-side capsule with no learner-meaningful content; #2/#3 didn't → #1 looked preselected/broken. MEASURED render path (both play route `ArenaPracticePlayClient` and Foundry test-preview `ArenaPracticeFlow` render `ArenaPracticePlayer`): its `ChoiceCard` rendered a right-side pill from a `tag` prop, and ONLY the ACTION-DECISION phase passed `tag={c.isActionCommitment ? t.actionTag("action"/"행동") : undefined}`. `isActionCommitment` is an INTERNAL authoring/generation classification (types.ts:85 "a real observable action commitment" vs waiting/preparing/deferring); the generation contract guarantees ≥1 commitment (typically the first) → that one card uniquely showed a lone pill. PRODUCT PURPOSE for the learner: NONE, and actively harmful — surfacing it leaks which option is the "real action" (preferred-answer / hidden-direction signal the directive forbids), and on mobile the tiny pill reads as an empty/meaningless capsule. Primary/tradeoff phases passed no tag (hence no pill there); EliteArenaStep2Context renders no choice pill → the action-tag was the sole capsule. Fix (inner-main `8109e0c2`, smallest correct, VISUAL ONLY): removed the `tag` prop + capsule markup from `ChoiceCard` and the `tag={...}` from the action phase, and deleted the now-unused `actionTag` copy (en/ko). ChoiceCard is now label-only, identical structure for every option in every phase; no index special-casing, no preselection, no new selection state — selection stays expressed only by advancing the phase. Accessibility preserved (choices remain native `<button>`s with label as accessible name). Scenario JSON, choice-submission API, practice reducer/progression, scoring, XP, QR, pattern, persistence UNTOUCHED. Verification: `tsc` clean; **+11 rendered tests** (REPRODUCTION that FAILS pre-fix [found `<span>action</span>` on choice 1]; no pill on any DECIDE choice; equivalent structure + no aria-pressed/selected; pill-free primary+tradeoff; no hidden score/correctness/XP/commitment leak; accessible-name; full-width tap target; first/second selection identical→complete; full progression + completion-once; test-mode no completion; KO surface pill-free); Arena+Foundry suite **853** green; full suite only the 16 documented pre-existing failures (4858 pass) — ZERO new regressions; build OK. Served-code: player chunk `page-1f4ac291ed83fafb.js` (200) retains "It gets harder"/"상황이 더 어려워집니다"/"Practice complete" (correct build live); the `actionTag` key (present ONLY for the removed pill) is ABSENT from the served bundle + all deployed chunks. **RESIDUAL = Commander ARENA CHOICE VISUAL gate (rule: an unselected choice must never look preselected or contain a control with no visible meaning):** cold-launch → open published Arena practice → reach 3-choice DECIDE → before tapping confirm NO empty capsule + #1 not preselected → tap #1 → selection clear/meaningful (advances) → progression works. **2026-07-17 — Foundry PDF PARTICIPANT NEXT PROGRESSION FIX (Slice 2.3A.2) — one tap now advances 1/2→2/2; stale-closure root cause; DEPLOYED; COMMANDER PDF NEXT DEVICE GATE PENDING.**
[Foundry PDF Next] Confirmed device finding: on a published (and legacy) 2-page PDF room the participant's Next button looked enabled but ONE tap left the reader stuck on 1/2 — page 2 never rendered, no error. ROOT CAUSE (definitive, A/B-proven): a stale-closure bug in `PdfReader.goNext` — `useCallback(() => setPage(p => Math.min(numPages || p, p + 1)), [])`. The EMPTY dep array captured `numPages=0` from the first render (before the PDF loaded), so `numPages || p` collapsed to `p` and the page never advanced; meanwhile the button's `disabled` check reads the FRESH render-scope numPages (=2) → looked enabled = an enabled no-op tap. (goPrev doesn't read numPages → Back unaffected.) The shared `PdfReader` means EVERY multi-page PDF event was affected, not just published — the participant read path (`resolveDocumentForRead` → `foundry_event_document_content.storage_path`) is identical for published (draft-referenced object) and legacy events (STEP 0 item 11/15: no identity difference). FIX (inner `b977adaf`, 3 files: PdfReader.tsx + FoundryDocumentClient.tsx + new PdfReader.test.tsx): `goNext` now depends on `[numPages]` and clamps explicitly (`numPages>0 ? min(numPages,p+1) : p`) → one tap advances 1/2→2/2 and renders page 2; Next is visibly DISABLED while the doc is still loading (numPages unknown) — never an enabled button that ignores the tap. UX: the control reads "Next page" on non-final pages and, on the final page ONCE the SERVER marks reading_complete, becomes "Continue to reflection" which scrolls the (already server-unlocked) reflection into view — completion stays server-authoritative, nothing faked client-side. UNTOUCHED: YouTube participant flow, publish/approval/snapshot/QR/token, XP, event history, reading-progress heartbeats + gate, completion API, Today/Center/Arena/Me, bty-karaoke. Verification: `tsc` clean; new **PdfReader test is an A/B-verified regression guard** (with the old empty-deps goNext the advance test FAILS "2 / 2" not found = the exact device symptom; with the fix it passes); foundry + /f suite **670/670** green; full suite only the 16 pre-existing failures — ZERO new regressions; build OK; diff = exactly 3 `/f/[token]` files (YouTube path untouched); secret scan clean. Deploy PASS `bty-arena-staging` Version `ea235565-e691-4eba-824f-c210148c1a8e` (100% active, 2026-07-17T04:50). BUILD_ID `99kbUlEYoaoehu3Wk1TNM`. Served-code verified: deployed `/f/[token]` chunk `page-4e7aebc0e4faa592.js` (200) contains "Next page" + "Continue to reflection" + ko "성찰로 계속". **Live-smoke RESIDUAL = Commander PDF-Next device gate (REQUIRED ORDER):** on the employee device — open the 2-page PDF room (1/2) → tap "Next page" once → document changes to page 2, counter 2/2, progress 2 of 2 pages read → control becomes "Continue to reflection" → tap it → completion question appears → submit → Training Complete + XP rules → reload restores state. FAIL if an apparently-enabled Next produces no change on one tap. Verdict: DEPLOYED — COMMANDER PDF NEXT DEVICE GATE PENDING.

**2026-07-15 — Foundry Living Reflection QUALITY GUARD V1 (mirror, not watch-behavior report); deployed to staging; COMMANDER EMPLOYEE DEVICE GATE PENDING.**

Inner commit `81121127` (6 files, +378/−118): (1) **domain validator** `living-reflection.ts` — `validateLivingReflection(input, {questionExcerpt?})` now rejects third-person employee refs (`the participant/user/employee`, `participant('s)`), watch-behavior-as-verdict (`engage*`, `conscious`, `chose/choice to`, `avoid*`, `skip*`, `lack*`, `effort`, `not fully engaged`, `watched less/more`), character claims (`your personality`, `this shows you`, `you are a/an/the`, `you tend to`), praise, `correct/right/wrong answer` + `you're correct/wrong`, generic coaching (`consider how`, `think about how`, `you should`, `make sure`, `clear communication`); rejects a living-sentence < 4 words (bare-fragment gate), four sections that restate one idea (significant-token Jaccard > 0.6), and the host question repeated verbatim across ≥2 sections. **2026-07-04 — STEP 5.2b-fix — Gate entry-light recede on Today arrival. Inner commit `33bb473a`; deploy HELD (Commander GO required).**

**On-device finding (frame-verified):** the golden entry light receded on a commit-anchored timer, completing BEFORE navigation landed → `/start` (blue + Orb) was re-revealed, then a hard cut to Today. Root cause = time-anchored recede, not arrival-anchored.

**Fix (`orbGoldenOverlay.ts` only):** after commit the overlay **HOLDS at PEAK** (no fade); the body-mounted singleton **polls `location.pathname` (~50ms, no Today coupling)** until it reaches `/today`, then **settles ~150ms** (Today paints under the light) and **recedes** (`ease-out`; warm gold hue fixed in the gradient → only opacity falls, no white/grey shift). The removed piece was the immediate double-rAF commit-time fade. **HARD FAILSAFE unchanged** — commit-anchored ~4s force-remove + `console.warn` still fires if arrival never detected (nav failure / auth redirect) or recede interrupted (`finalize` now also clears the arrival/settle timers).

**Scope:** `orbGoldenOverlay.ts` only. **OrbLiving / Today / routing untouched**; all prior 5.2b locks intact.

**Gates:** tsc PASS; build PASS (306/306); terminology net-increase 0. Inner pushed `b41360dc..33bb473a` → origin/inner-main.

**Deploy:** **HELD** — Commander GO required. **UNVERIFIED-BY-CODE (real-device):** no `/start` re-reveal between commit and Today; smooth hold→recede over Today; no stuck overlay (watch for the failsafe `console.warn`).

**2026-07-04 — STEP 5.2a NOTE — wave origin source confirmed = Orb element; 5.2b radius caveat recorded. (docs-only)**

**Origin source (Commander Q):** the wave origin is measured from the **Orb canvas element** (`canvasRef.current`, `OrbLiving.tsx:714` `canvas.getBoundingClientRect()`), **NOT** the body-mounted field canvas (`fieldCanvas.getBoundingClientRect` = none). → answer **(a)**. Because `getBoundingClientRect()` includes transforms, the origin stays centered on the Orb even under a future Orb scale/move — no origin-source change needed before 5.2b. (The 5.0 regression was the *drawing surface* offset by a transformed ancestor, not the origin measurement; 5.2a fixed the surface.)

**5.2b caveat (radius, NOT origin — record now, fix later):** the ring **start radius** uses the constant logical `orbR = size*0.42` (`OrbLiving.tsx:641` `R = orbR + FIELD_RING_W + …`). If 5.2b scales the Orb, the center stays correct but the ring would start at the *unscaled* radius → **inside** the enlarged Orb. **5.2b fix:** derive the start radius from the measured Orb rect (`r.width/2`) so the wave still emits from the *scaled* boundary. Origin needs no change. Not this track; recorded for 5.2b.

**2026-07-04 — STEP 5.2a — Orb wave portal stabilization. Inner commit `0637b9fb`; deploy HELD (Commander GO required).**

**What:** moved OrbLiving's outward-wave canvas out of the Orb wrapper and mounted it **directly on `document.body`** (imperative `createElement`+`appendChild` in the existing effect; `fieldCanvas.remove()` on unmount). `OrbLiving.tsx` only (1 file, 16+/18−). **NOT deployed.**

**Why:** STEP 5.0 diag — a transform on the `/start` Orb wrapper made the fixed wave canvas (then a *descendant*) resolve to the 220px wrapper instead of the viewport → wave drifted to 좌하단 + layer edges showed. Prepares for the future hold-to-enter Orb scale-up without origin drift.

**Transform immunity:** the wave layer now has **no transformed ancestor** (body child), so a future Orb scale/transform cannot become its containing block. Wave origin stays in **viewport space** via `canvas.getBoundingClientRect()` (getBoundingClientRect already accounts for transforms) → centered on the Orb now, and correct even under a future Orb scale.

**Preserved (0-change):** wave draw path + all constants (`FIELD_WAVE_A 0.09`, `FIELD_MAX_TRAVEL orbR*4.6`, ring easing) unchanged → accepted outward-wave feel intact; `orbHaptic` untouched (haptic timing/exclusivity unchanged); `pointer-events:none` (no touch intercept); transparent except fading rings (no rectangular bounds). **NOT done:** hold-to-enter, Orb scale-up, Today transition (all deferred).

**Gates:** tsc PASS; build PASS (306/306); terminology net-increase 0 (OrbLiving not flagged). Inner pushed `4c8bdc01..0637b9fb` → origin/inner-main.

**Deploy:** **HELD** — awaiting **real-device iPhone Native Sensory Gate + explicit Commander GO** (this step changes wave render structure; on-device is the deploy criterion). Manual sensory checklist (tap/hold haptic, wave-from-Orb, outward, no random/lower-left/rectangular origin) = **UNVERIFIED-BY-CODE**; code-level checks (transform immunity, viewport origin, constants/haptic unchanged) confirmed.

**2026-07-04 — STEP 5.1 — Restore Orb outward wave baseline (revert fea92eb5). Inner-only; deploy HELD.**

**What:** reverted `fea92eb5` (STEP 5 Orb→Today transition) on `inner-main` — inner revert commit **`4c8bdc01`** (2 files, 9+/85−; symmetric reverse). NOT deployed.

**Root cause (STEP 5.0 read-only diag):** fea92eb5 added `transform: scale(1)` + `will-change: transform` to the `/start` Orb wrapper `<div role="button">` — an ancestor of OrbLiving's `position:fixed` field-wave canvas. A non-`none` transform (and, independently, `will-change: transform`) makes that element the **containing block** for fixed descendants, so the full-viewport wave canvas re-anchored to the 220px wrapper (screen-centre) instead of the viewport → outward wave landed offset (좌하단) + the layer's edges showed on screen. Present even at idle (transform/will-change applied when not exiting). At the approved baseline `ceb3163f` no such ancestor transform existed. Wave origin = `canvas.getBoundingClientRect()` centre (viewport coords) drawn into a canvas whose origin had shifted → mismatch.

**Restored:** `/start` back to plain `router.push('/{locale}/today')` (no wrapper transform/will-change); Today `?enter=orb` light-echo removed (STEP 5 소속, 함께 소멸). **OrbLiving.tsx untouched (0 diff)** — accepted Orb brightness/haptic/restrained-core/outward-wave baseline intact. Hold-to-enter **deferred** (not in this commit).

**Gates:** tsc PASS; terminology 14 flagged = pre-existing baseline, my 2 files contribute 0 → net-increase 0. Inner pushed `fea92eb5..4c8bdc01` → origin/inner-main (remote == local).

**Deploy:** **DONE** (Commander GO 2026-07-04) — `bty-arena-staging` Version **`b6d1ecdc-6a25-4d34-af5e-79f1cccf76f4`**, HEAD `4c8bdc01`. 3-way fresh: served `/start` chunk no longer contains `scale(1.06)`/`enter=orb` (offending transform + echo gone → fixed wave canvas re-anchored to viewport); OrbLiving chunk still carries Haptics + the `pointer-events:none` field layer. `/start` 200, `/en/dev/orb` 200.

**Next (when STEP 5 transition is re-introduced):** adopt **Option B1 — portal the field-wave canvas to `document.body`** so `position:fixed` stays viewport-anchored regardless of any transformed ancestor. Do NOT re-add a transform on a fixed-canvas ancestor.

**2026-07-03 — STEP A2 — Native Durable Session Restore CLOSED.**

**Status:** CLOSED. Inner commit `71af9192`; deploy `bty-arena-staging` `06a5ec38-c287-4f62-951d-b5c6c1c3dfe2`.

**Purpose:** solve the WKWebView async cookie-persistence race on native app hard-kill.

**Root cause:** Supabase auth cookies were persistent in principle, but WKWebView's disk flush is asynchronous — an immediate app kill after login could lose the auth cookie before it reached disk, and no native durable copy existed. STEP A (session-gate refresh) could not help this case (no refresh_token cookie survived to refresh).

**Fix:** Keychain-backed native durable session restore. Plugin `capacitor-secure-storage-plugin@0.13.0` (iOS Keychain); key `bty.session.v1`. All web glue is `isNative()`-gated; server routes reused unchanged.

**Behavior:** (1) native login stores session material in Keychain before redirect; (2) native launch restore reads Keychain when the normal session gate is unauthenticated; (3) restore re-establishes the session without Google login; (4) logout clears Keychain before restore can run.

**Verification:** (1) app kill after login → relaunch → Google login does NOT appear → Orb appears: **PASS**; (2) logout → relaunch → Google login required: **PASS**.

**Scope:** web behavior unchanged · server routes unchanged · auth/callback untouched · PKCE untouched · legacy JWT untouched · Orb / Foundry / Today / Arena / Center / BottomNav untouched · no DB migration · no schema change.

**Final product meaning:** Google OAuth remains account binding/recovery; daily relaunch now restores silently and enters Orb; the first major Daily Entry v1 blocker is resolved.

"GRANT 안 함" ≠ "service-role only". service-role-only는 **REVOKE EXECUTE FROM public, anon, authenticated**로만 성립. 향후 모든 Reality-award definer fn = revoke 필수. 교훈: runtime ACL 실측이 보안 추론 위 권위("verify-then-no-op", catalog가 권위) — 이번에 commit-message/추론(=service-role only 가정)이 stale, runtime이 진실이었다.

Flow: requireUser → requireApprovedMembership → verifyEventQrToken(btyev1) → SELECT bty_events → cancelled(409)/valid_until(410 DB재읽기) → INSERT participation(service-role) → 23505→200 already_scanned / fresh→RPC-atomic core add → 200. **2026-06-24 — Award 형태(2b).** RPC-atomic increment of `core_xp_total`(insert+core add 단일 tx; award 실패=insert 롤백). derived reprojection = post-tx best-effort, self-healing(transient 실패가 XP 손실 아님). `applyDirectCoreXp` increment-half = RPC 이주, recompute-half만 미러.

**2026-06-24 — Core XP 권위 + Reality Engine SDK 계약(canon).** Core XP의 권위 = RPC tx가 반환한 `new_core_xp`. 모든 파생 계산은 그 값을 입력으로만 사용. 재조회·재계산 금지. 공통 계약: `award() → returns new_core_xp → reproject(new_core_xp)`. Action·Event·Learning·Mission·Volunteer = 동일 인터페이스. award=Core 확정(RPC-atomic), reproject=확정값 투영(계산 아님).

**2026-06-24 — Reality Engine 책임 경계(canon).** Reality Engine은 성장을 계산하지 않는다. 검증하고 Core XP를 제출한다. 성장 계산은 Core XP 이후에만 일어난다.

**2026-06-24 — Root Rule(canon, slice 위).** Verified Reality → `applyDirectCoreXp()` 등가(=RPC-atomic) → Core XP → {Avatar·Identity·TII·Leaderboard·Season·Weekly·Code·Level} = 전부 파생 View. Core XP = Root, 나머지 = derived. D1 = Direct Core XP 확정(철학: 현실이 Arena를 키운다). `activity_xp_event_id` NULL 잔존(미래 연결 가능, shared schema 무수정).

**2026-06-24 — slice-1 runtime catalog 실측(stale 교정).** commit `3135f300` 메시지 "not yet applied" = known-stale. runtime 실측: `bty_event_participation_unique` UNIQUE `(event_id,user_id)` + pkey **live**. slice-1 테이블/제약 = applied. unique = Reality Engine 무결성 핵심 장치(no-cap direct-Core의 dup-inflation 차단점).

## 2026-06-02 — [SECURITY] REVOKE anon EXECUTE on 6 SECURITY DEFINER funcs — advisor WARN — APPLIED to prod
- Migration `bty-app/supabase/migrations/20260602000002_revoke_anon_execute_definer_funcs.sql` 적용 완료 (6 × `DO $$` to_regprocedure 가드 + GRANT-then-REVOKE).
- 봉쇄: DEFINER 함수가 caller RLS 우회 → anon EXECUTE(직접+PUBLIC)면 미인증자가 XP 조작/profile/season RPC 직접 호출 = 권한상승.
- caller 분류(코드 검증, 전부 auth-gated): authenticated 4 (`increment_arena_xp`/`increment_weekly_xp`/`ensure_arena_profile`/`consume_lab_attempt`) + service_role 2 (`run_season_carryover`/`get_leaderboard_profiles`).
- fix: 4개 → GRANT TO authenticated,service_role 후 REVOKE FROM anon,PUBLIC; 2개 → GRANT TO service_role 후 REVOKE FROM anon,authenticated,PUBLIC.
- **★ GRANT-then-REVOKE**: 관측상 접근이 PUBLIC 경유 → bare REVOKE FROM PUBLIC이 authenticated/service_role까지 끊을 위험 → 명시 GRANT 선행으로 ACL 구조 무관 안전. grant-only, XP 로직/불변식 무변경.
- verify: db push `Finished`(0 error) / migration list 동기화 / ACL-after = pg_proc proacl 재쿼리 Commander 전달(anon 6개 제거 / authenticated 4 유지 / service_role 2 단독).
- PENDING: 코어루프 smoke(RLS smoke와 합침) — authenticated XP 정상이면 REVOKE+RLS 둘 다 무영향. NOTE: leaderboard security_definer_view = Commander가 이미 drop(view_count=0).

## LRI/Certified — Strategy B pending-pulse endpoint (B-1, 2026-06-01)

## assessment 50문항 게이트 (race + 미완제출 + 4xx 노출) — CLOSED (2026-05-30)
- 증상: 50문항 자존감 진단 제출이 "answers_count_mismatch: expected 50, got 31"
  400으로 거부 → "No result found / Go to assessment" fallback. 28일 train은
  이 진단 통과 후 진입하는 게이트라, 진단 실패 시 train day(mark-complete)까지
  도달 불가. (train mark-complete 코드는 assessment와 독립 — 별개 게이트 문제.)
- 근본원인(L2-0 진단):
  · race(B): AssessmentClient 옵션 onClick auto-advance setTimeout(280ms) +
    bottom Next + goPrev 세 nav 경로가 advance 가드 없음 → 빠른/중복 클릭 시
    setCurrentIndex 다중 큐잉 → 문항 skip → 미답 → answeredCount<50.
    회귀 아님(a746c07a부터 잠재, 타이밍 의존). 데이터 정상(en/ko 둘 다 50).
  · 미완제출(A): canSubmit(answeredCount===total) 계산되나 미사용(dead code).
    submit 버튼 disabled={!canGoNext}로 현재 문항만 확인 → 미완 payload 서버 도달.
  · silent(C): res.ok 실패해도 무조건 result push → 무효 데이터 client 오결과.
- 수정(코드 1파일 AssessmentClient.tsx, 서버 strict-50 보존):
  · B: 세 nav 경로 단일 autoAdvanceTimeoutRef 공유·상호 clear + unmount cleanup
    → pending advance 최대 1개, skip 구조적 불가.
  · A: 버튼 disabled isLast 분기(!canSubmit) + handler answeredCount!==total
    가드(서버 호출 전) + N/total 미완 안내.
  · C: 4xx(validation 거부)는 push 차단 + submitError alert(mismatch 친화 안내).
    5xx/network는 의도된 오프라인 client 채점 fallback 보존(result/ResultClient
    client-side scoring) — push 유지.
- test: AssessmentClient.test.tsx 신규 5케이스(완주/race/미완/4xx/5xx-fallback).
  race 가드(B)와 5xx fallback 보존(C)이 회귀 lock.
- 검증: tsc 0 / lint 0 / vitest 3398/0/6 (3393 +5 신규).
- 효과: 정상 사용자 50문항 완주→제출 / 빠른클릭 skip 불가 / 미완 submit 차단+안내
  / 4xx 노출 / 5xx fallback 보존.
- 배포: 코드 inner 8bfe4588. deploy 별도(다음).
- carry-forward: (이전) train_day_completions CREATE TABLE drift / CoachChatPane
  non-contiguous semantic. (신규 없음 — 이 lane은 단일 파일 클로즈.)

## arena_level_records drift — RESOLVED DB-side (column-name mismatch, no repo commit) (D-4 · 2026-05-29)

**Active head (D-4 / 2026-05-29):** no code commit (DB-only hotfix); worker `44751b0b-6ad7-4ddf-aff2-2102d94c8385` unchanged (already expected the code column names). Commander SQL hotfix applied out-of-band. **Working tree:** clean (both repos); the staged STEP-1 migration file was discarded.

**arena_level_records drift**: [x] **완료 (DB-side).** Symptom: `qr/validate` level record update failed at runtime ("column does not exist") → `consecutive_verified_completions` never incremented = **band progression silently stalled** (verify / run-done / XP / AIR all succeeded). **Root cause:** the live table diverged from the CREATE TABLE migration (`20260431240000`) — the real column was **`last_evaluated_at` (DB)** vs **`last_evaluation_at` (code-expected)**; a column-NAME mismatch, not a simple absence. Further divergence noted: PK `id` (DB) vs `user_id` (migration), `integer` vs `smallint`, default `'L'` vs `'mid'` — the table was created **outside the migration path**. **Fix (Commander SQL hotfix, no redeploy):** (1) ADD COLUMN `last_band_change_at` (genuinely absent); (2) a combined `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` attempt **created** `last_evaluation_at` as a NEW column → duplicate (`last_evaluated_at` + `last_evaluation_at` coexisting); (3) diagnosed as a name mismatch — confirmed `last_evaluated_at` all-NULL → `DROP COLUMN last_evaluated_at`; (4) table column names now match the code; verified via `information_schema`. **Verified GREEN (Commander tail 9:08:45):** `qr/validate` → `awaitingVerification:true` → `arena_run_done_after_contract_verify`; "level record update failed" gone.

**Closure 판정:** level_records drift RESOLVED DB-side (runtime green). Repo-migration realignment deferred (separate, post-launch).

**Lesson (recorded):** prescribing "add the missing column" **without `information_schema` ground-truth** nearly worsened a column-NAME mismatch into a **duplicate column** — `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` does not match a differently-named existing column, it creates a new one. **Verify the real schema, then mutate.** (The earlier STEP-0 diagnosis inferred absence from the runtime error alone; the live-schema query was the missing step.)

**Carry-forward (updated):**
- **★ arena_level_records repo-migration realignment** [post-launch] — the live DB differs from the CREATE TABLE migration (column names / PK / types / defaults). A separate alignment migration must reconcile the repo to the actual DB state. **Do NOT edit the original CREATE TABLE (`20260431240000`)** — forward alignment migration only. The discarded STEP-1 file (single-column, didn't reflect this divergence) is not a usable base.
- **★ 안2-B (multi-QR session surface)** [Commander launch target] + **★ #1 scan-confirmation UI** (bundle).
- EN T3 (28일 train body KO, score.ts, root layout lang, not-found, train/28days, auth callback:168).
